Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The wind variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 529 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 879 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by wind generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 529, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 879. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any wind module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 68. The projective resolution has length at most 205 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the wind decomposition. By minimality, the element a^68 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 205-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
